
============================================================
=== FINAL GTIR HANDED TO GTFN BACKEND ===
============================================================
__field_operator_compute_zavgS(pp, S_M, out) {
  compute_zavgS = λ(pp, S_M) → 
    (λ(zavgᐞ0) → (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(S_M, zavgᐞ0))(
      (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
        0.5, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
               (⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2Vₒ, 0ₒ⟫(__it)))(pp), (⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(__it)))(pp)
             )
      )
    );
  out @ u⟨ Edgeₕ: [get_domain_range(out, Edgeₕ)[0], get_domain_range(out, Edgeₕ)[1][ ⟩
       ← compute_zavgS(pp, S_M);
}

============================================================
=== GTIR AFTER INLINING LIFTS AND LAMBDAS ===
============================================================
__field_operator_compute_zavgS(pp, S_M, out) {
  out @ c⟨ IDimₕ: [1ₒ, 12ₒ[, JDimₕ: [1ₒ, 10ₒ[, Kolorₕ: [0ₒ, 3ₒ[ ⟩
       ← as_fieldop(
           λ(S_M, __iasfop_30, pp) → 
             (λ(_cs_14, _cs_16) → 
                ((if   _cs_16
                  then if   True ∧ _cs_14 < 1
                       then ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(pp)
                       else if   2 <= _cs_14 ∧ True
                            then ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(pp)
                            else ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -1ₒ⟫(pp)
                  else if 1 <= _cs_14 ∧ True then if   True ∧ _cs_14 < 1
                                                  then ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(pp)
                                                  else if   2 <= _cs_14 ∧ True
                                                       then ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(pp)
                                                       else ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -1ₒ⟫(pp) else ·pp)
                 + (if   _cs_16
                    then if   True ∧ _cs_14 < 1
                         then ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(pp)
                         else if 2 <= _cs_14 ∧ True then ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(pp) else ·⟪_OffKolorₒ, -1ₒ⟫(pp)
                    else if   1 <= _cs_14 ∧ True
                         then if   True ∧ _cs_14 < 1
                              then ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(pp)
                              else if 2 <= _cs_14 ∧ True then ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(pp) else ·⟪_OffKolorₒ, -1ₒ⟫(pp)
                         else ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ⟫(pp))) × 0.5 × ·S_M)(·__iasfop_30, True ∧ ·__iasfop_30 < 0),
           c⟨ IDimₕ: [1ₒ, 12ₒ[, JDimₕ: [1ₒ, 10ₒ[, Kolorₕ: [0ₒ, 3ₒ[ ⟩
         )(S_M, index(Kolorₕ), pp);
}

__field_operator_compute_pnabla(pp, S_M, sign, vol, out) {
  compute_zavgS = λ(pp, S_M) → 
    (λ(zavgᐞ0) → (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(S_M, zavgᐞ0))(
      (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
        0.5, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
               (⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2Vₒ, 0ₒ⟫(__it)))(pp), (⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(__it)))(pp)
             )
      )
    );
  compute_pnabla = λ(pp, S_M, sign, vol) → 
    (λ(zavgSᐞ0) → (λ(pnabla_Mᐞ0) → (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 / ·__arg1))(pnabla_Mᐞ0, vol))(
                    (⇑(λ(__arg0) → reduce(plus, 0)(·__arg0)))(
                      (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → map_(multiplies)(·__arg0, ·__arg1)))(
                        (⇑(λ(it) → neighbors(V2Eₒ, it)))(zavgSᐞ0), sign
                      )
                    )
                  ))(compute_zavgS(pp, S_M));
  out @ u⟨ Vertexₕ: [get_domain_range(out, Vertexₕ)[0], get_domain_range(out, Vertexₕ)[1][ ⟩
       ← compute_pnabla(pp, S_M, sign, vol);
}

============================================================
=== GTIR AFTER INLINING LIFTS AND LAMBDAS ===
============================================================
__field_operator_compute_pnabla(pp, S_M, sign, vol, out) {
  out @ c⟨ IDimₕ: [1ₒ, 12ₒ[, JDimₕ: [1ₒ, 10ₒ[, Kolorₕ: [0ₒ, 1ₒ[ ⟩

                    + (·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -1ₒ⟫(_cs_100) + ·⟪_OffKolorₒ, -1ₒ⟫(_cs_100)) × 0.5
                      × ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 1ₒ⟫(S_M) × list_get(4, ·sign)
                    + (·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(_cs_99) + ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(_cs_99)) × 0.5
                      × ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 2ₒ⟫(S_M) × list_get(5, ·sign)) / ·vol)(
                  ⟪_OffIDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 2ₒ⟫(pp),
                  ⟪_OffIDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 1ₒ⟫(pp),
                  ⟪_OffJDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 2ₒ⟫(pp),
                  True ∧ _cs_112 < 0,
                  ⟪_OffJDimₒ, -1ₒ⟫(pp),
                  ⟪_OffKolorₒ, 1ₒ⟫(pp),
                  True ∧ _cs_113 < 0
                ))(·⟪_OffJDimₒ, -1ₒ⟫(__iasfop_129), ·__iasfop_129),
           c⟨ IDimₕ: [1ₒ, 12ₒ[, JDimₕ: [1ₒ, 10ₒ[, Kolorₕ: [0ₒ, 1ₒ[ ⟩
         )(S_M, index(Kolorₕ), pp, sign, vol);
}

=== GTIR END ===

__field_operator_compute_pnabla(pp, S_M, sign, vol, out) {
  out @ c⟨ IDimₕ: [1ₒ, 12ₒ[, JDimₕ: [1ₒ, 10ₒ[, Kolorₕ: [0ₒ, 1ₒ[ ⟩
       ← as_fieldop(
           λ(S_M, __iasfop_129, pp, sign, vol) → 
             (λ(_cs_112, _cs_113) → 
                (λ(_cs_99, _cs_100, _cs_101, _cs_104, _cs_105, _cs_106, _cs_109) → 
                   (((if   _cs_109
                      then ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(pp)
                      else if 1 <= _cs_113 ∧ True then ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(pp) else ·pp)
                     + (if   _cs_109
                        then ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(pp)
                        else if 1 <= _cs_113 ∧ True then ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(pp) else ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ⟫(pp)))
                    × 0.5 × ·S_M × list_get(0, ·sign)
                    + (·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -1ₒ⟫(_cs_106) + ·⟪_OffKolorₒ, -1ₒ⟫(_cs_106)) × 0.5
                      × ·⟪_OffKolorₒ, 1ₒ⟫(S_M) × list_get(1, ·sign)
                    + (·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(_cs_101) + ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(_cs_101)) × 0.5
                      × ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 2ₒ⟫(S_M) × list_get(2, ·sign)
                    + ((if   _cs_104
                        then ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(_cs_105)
                        else if 1 <= _cs_112 ∧ True then ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(_cs_105) else ·_cs_105)
                       + (if   _cs_104
                          then ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(_cs_105)
                          else if   1 <= _cs_112 ∧ True
                               then ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(_cs_105)
                               else ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ⟫(_cs_105))) × 0.5 × ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, -1ₒ⟫(S_M) × list_get(3, ·sign)
                    + (·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -1ₒ⟫(_cs_100) + ·⟪_OffKolorₒ, -1ₒ⟫(_cs_100)) × 0.5
                      × ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 1ₒ⟫(S_M) × list_get(4, ·sign)
                    + (·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(_cs_99) + ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(_cs_99)) × 0.5
                      × ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 2ₒ⟫(S_M) × list_get(5, ·sign)) / ·vol)(
                  ⟪_OffIDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 2ₒ⟫(pp),
                  ⟪_OffIDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 1ₒ⟫(pp),
                  ⟪_OffJDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 2ₒ⟫(pp),
                  True ∧ _cs_112 < 0,
                  ⟪_OffJDimₒ, -1ₒ⟫(pp),
                  ⟪_OffKolorₒ, 1ₒ⟫(pp),
                  True ∧ _cs_113 < 0
                ))(·⟪_OffJDimₒ, -1ₒ⟫(__iasfop_129), ·__iasfop_129),
           c⟨ IDimₕ: [1ₒ, 12ₒ[, JDimₕ: [1ₒ, 10ₒ[, Kolorₕ: [0ₒ, 1ₒ[ ⟩
         )(S_M, index(Kolorₕ), pp, sign, vol);
}

                      then ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(pp)
                      else if 1 <= _cs_113 ∧ True then ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(pp) else ·pp)

                        else if 1 <= _cs_112 ∧ True then ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(_cs_105) else ·_cs_105)

__field_operator_compute_neighbor_sum_unweighted(zavgS, out) {
  compute_neighbor_sum_unweighted = λ(zavgS) → 
    (⇑(λ(__arg0) → reduce(plus, 0)(·__arg0)))((⇑(λ(it) → neighbors(V2Eₒ, it)))(zavgS));
  out @ u⟨ Vertexₕ: [get_domain_range(out, Vertexₕ)[0], get_domain_range(out, Vertexₕ)[1][ ⟩
       ← compute_neighbor_sum_unweighted(zavgS);
}

============================================================
=== FINAL GTIR HANDED TO GTFN BACKEND ===
============================================================
__field_operator_compute_neighbor_sum_weighted(zavgS, sign, out) {
  compute_neighbor_sum_weighted = λ(zavgS, sign) → 
    (⇑(λ(__arg0) → reduce(plus, 0)(·__arg0)))(
      (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → map_(multiplies)(·__arg0, ·__arg1)))(
        (⇑(λ(it) → neighbors(V2Eₒ, it)))(zavgS), sign
      )
    );
  out @ u⟨ Vertexₕ: [get_domain_range(out, Vertexₕ)[0], get_domain_range(out, Vertexₕ)[1][ ⟩
       ← compute_neighbor_sum_weighted(zavgS, sign);
}

    (⇑(λ(__arg0) → reduce(plus, 0)(·__arg0)))(
      (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → map_(multiplies)(·__arg0, ·__arg1)))(

                             )
                           ))(zavgS, sign);

__field_operator_compute_neighbor_sum_weighted(zavgS, sign, out) {

       ← as_fieldop(

                            + ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 2ₒ⟫(zavgS) × list_get(2, ·sign)
                            + ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, -1ₒ⟫(zavgS) × list_get(3, ·sign)
                            + ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 1ₒ⟫(zavgS) × list_get(4, ·sign)
                            + ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 2ₒ⟫(zavgS) × list_get(5, ·sign),
           c⟨ IDimₕ: [1ₒ, 12ₒ[, JDimₕ: [1ₒ, 10ₒ[, Kolorₕ: [0ₒ, 1ₒ[ ⟩
         )(zavgS, sign);

       ← as_fieldop(
           λ(zavgS, sign) → ·zavgS × list_get(0, ·sign) + ·⟪_OffKolorₒ, 1ₒ⟫(zavgS) × list_get(1, ·sign)
                            + ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 2ₒ⟫(zavgS) × list_get(2, ·sign)
                            + ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, -1ₒ⟫(zavgS) × list_get(3, ·sign)
                            + ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 1ₒ⟫(zavgS) × list_get(4, ·sign)
                            + ·⟪_OffIDimₒ, -1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, 2ₒ⟫(zavgS) × list_get(5, ·sign),
           c⟨ IDimₕ: [1ₒ, 12ₒ[, JDimₕ: [1ₒ, 10ₒ[, Kolorₕ: [0ₒ, 1ₒ[ ⟩
         )(zavgS, sign);

============================================================
=== FINAL GTIR HANDED TO GTFN BACKEND ===
============================================================
__field_operator_compute_divide_volume(pnabla_M, vol, out) {
  compute_divide_volume = λ(pnabla_M, vol) → 
    (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 / ·__arg1))(pnabla_M, vol);
  out @ c⟨ Vertexₕ: [get_domain_range(out, Vertexₕ)[0], get_domain_range(out, Vertexₕ)[1][ ⟩
       ← compute_divide_volume(pnabla_M, vol);
}
============================================================


============================================================
=== GTIR AFTER INLINING FUNDEFS ===
============================================================
__field_operator_compute_divide_volume(pnabla_M, vol, out) {
  compute_divide_volume = λ(pnabla_M, vol) → 
    (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 / ·__arg1))(pnabla_M, vol);
  out @ c⟨ Vertexₕ: [get_domain_range(out, Vertexₕ)[0], get_domain_range(out, Vertexₕ)[1][ ⟩
